# The L3 application dispatch's release — the site table (2026-11-12)

**What this file is.** The working file entry 767 §3 step 1 orders, with the part Ruling 1 of
`records/cowork/rulings/cowork_rulings_2026_11_12_l3_application_release_plan_sitting.md` (the plan ruling) adds. One
line per set of release edits: its ruling, the dispatch lines it names, the change in its own words (quoted), whether it
touches the working list, and the dispatch text at those lines as checked. **It decides nothing and edits nothing.**
Documentation and working practice only; no algorithm change. The dispatch is
`records/cc/instructions/cc_instruction_l3_application_2026_11_08.md` at 593,140 bytes; the list is
`ratification_surfaces/cowork_l3_application_release_points_2026_11_09.md` at 459,987. Line numbers are theirs at those
sizes. **[checked]** means read at the object by the sitting that wrote the line; **[relayed]** means not.

`records/cowork/rulings/cowork_rulings_2026_11_12_l3_site_table_working_practice_sitting.md`, as he extended it.)*

**The form.** One entry per occurrence of *"To do at release"* in the list, in list order, named **L-NNNN** by the list
line on which the phrase begins. Each entry gives the list item it belongs to, the instruction in its own words (quoted),
its mark, and the dispatch text at the lines it names, as checked. The marks: ***not yet ratified*** (the list's own
derivation or question, which no ruling has taken up; it waits for step 4, D-599); ***staleness: no ratification
needed*** (it only corrects a stale or wrong passage, the user's rule of 2026-09-22); ***taken up by a ruling*** (a
ruling block above orders the same edit or answers the question the instruction rests on; the block, not the list,
governs, and the block is named); ***not an instruction*** (the phrase is mentioned, not used). An instruction may carry
two marks, row by row.

**The count.** A search of the list for *"To do at release"* within one line meets 68 lines [checked]. The phrase also
breaks across a line end nine times, at list lines 681–682, 712–713, 1158–1159, 1515–1516, 1732–1733, 1790–1791,
1826–1827, 3010–3011 and 3153–3154 [checked by a search across lines], so the list carries **77** occurrences, and not
every one is an instruction (L-0492, L-0500). The plan ruling's *"68 occurrences (a count by search …)"* is the
one-line count; that record is not edited by this work, and the count here is the one to use.

  words; whether the act writes a line for the two parts only (form (b-H) allows a line naming a part) is to be
